Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the thermodynamics space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 680 and the convergence rate is O(184/n^4).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_680(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 680. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^184(S, F) is trivial. QED.
